# D:\Skills — Catálogo plano

Generado por `scratchpad/build_catalog.py`. Regenerar al añadir repos.

- **stitch** — Clean, high-contrast enterprise design for data-driven workflows with intuitive drag-and-drop patterns and structured layouts.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/stitch`
- **storytelling** — Narrative-driven design using visuals, copy, and interaction to guide users through engaging, emotionally resonant journeys.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/storytelling`
- **terracotta** — A sun-baked, clay-toned editorial interface built on warm cream surfaces, ink-brown headlines set in a display serif, and a single terracotta accent.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/terracotta`
- **tetris** — Classic block-game inspired design with playful colors, bold display fonts, and compact, high-energy layouts.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/tetris`
- **vibrant** — Lively, colorful design with bold playful typography, warm accents, and dynamic visual energy.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/vibrant`
- **vintage** — 1950s-1990s nostalgia with skeuomorphic touches, grainy textures, retro color palettes, and pixel-style typography.  · `awesome-design-skills/skills/vintage`

- **cavecrew** — When to delegate to `cavecrew-investigator` (locate code), `cavecrew-builder` (1-2 file edit) or `cavecrew-reviewer` (diff review) instead of working inline or using `Explore`. Their output is compressed, so main contex…  · `caveman/skills/cavecrew`

- **caveman-help** — Quick-reference card for caveman modes, skills and commands. Trigger: /caveman-help or "caveman help".  · `caveman/skills/caveman-help`
- **caveman-learn** — Act on a Caveman learn report - review the ranked token sinks, apply cost-lowering fixes with per-edit consent, and report what those fixes returned. Use when asked to lower an agent's token cost, what caveman has saved…  · `caveman/skills/caveman-learn`
- **caveman-manage** — Inspect Caveman Cloud's experiment lifecycle and block unsafe execution. Use when asked to start, approve, cancel, promote or roll back a Caveman experiment.  · `caveman/skills/caveman-manage`
- **caveman-optimize** — Turn a Caveman optimization observation into an operator-chosen candidate with a paired baseline evaluation. Use when asked to inspect or evaluate a Caveman optimization report. Needs explicit approval.  · `caveman/skills/caveman-optimize`
- **caveman-review** — Compressed code review - one line per finding with location, problem and fix. Use for /caveman-review, "review this PR", or "review the diff".  · `caveman/skills/caveman-review`
- **caveman-setup** — Wire a repository through the Caveman Cloud gateway so every LLM request is measured, with no behavior change. Use for "set up caveman" or adding LLM spend observability.  · `caveman/skills/caveman-setup`

- **investigate-first** — Diagnose ambiguous failures before editing. Use for unknown causes, intermittent behavior, performance regressions, or investigations needing evidence-ranked hypotheses.  · `caveman/skills/investigate-first`
- **lean-build** — Build feature work with high overbuilding risk. Use for new behavior, product slices, or integrations where repository reuse, strict scope, and an explicit stop condition matter.  · `caveman/skills/lean-build`
- **migration** — Implement reversible compatibility-safe transitions. Use for schema, data, API, protocol, configuration, or dependency migrations requiring rollback and preservation proof.  · `caveman/skills/migration`
- **safe-refactor** — Restructure code while preserving behavior. Use for extraction, consolidation, ownership moves, or cleanup where verification must bracket structural edits.  · `caveman/skills/safe-refactor`

## Papercraft, troquelado & fabricación (9 skills + 4 tools)

_Batch 2026-10-26. Skills: openscad-skills (joaorura, MIT; escritas para Gemini CLI, valen igual) y cadquery-skills (gAmUssA, MIT; plugin de Claude Code) para CAD paramétrico → proyección 2D SVG/DXF con tolerancias. Tools (sin SKILL.md, se usan directamente): Export-Paper-Model-from-Blender (add-on, despliega mallas con pestañas/pliegues → SVG/PDF; manejable vía Blender MCP), boxes (Boxes.py CLI, cajas de corte láser con finger joints/kerf), packbox y packaging-dieline-generator (dielines de empaques → SVG/PDF)._

- **openscad-boardgame-organizer** — Use when creating board game inserts or organizers using The Boardgame Insert Toolkit (BIT).  _[openscad-skills]_  · `openscad-skills/openscad-boardgame-organizer`
- **openscad-bosl2-mastery** — Use when Gemini CLI is implementing geometry using the BOSL2 library.  _[openscad-skills]_  · `openscad-skills/openscad-bosl2-mastery`
- **openscad-project-understanding** — Use when Gemini CLI needs to structure, organize, or manage a complex OpenSCAD project.  _[openscad-skills]_  · `openscad-skills/openscad-project-understanding`
- **openscad-workflow-planning** — Use when planning a new OpenSCAD model or refactoring an existing one for better parametric control.  _[openscad-skills]_  · `openscad-skills/openscad-workflow-planning`
- **assembly-constraints** — Create and manage CadQuery assemblies with parts, sub-assemblies, and constraint-based positioning.  _[cadquery-skills]_  · `cadquery-skills/skills/assembly-constraints`
- **cad-code-generation** — Create or edit runnable parametric CadQuery Python models for parts and assemblies, including manufacturable details and existing-feature preservation.  _[cadquery-skills]_  · `cadquery-skills/skills/cad-code-generation`
- **cad-planner** — You are the Chief Engineer. You design machines by recursively subdividing them into assemblies and parts according to the filesystem architecture.  _[cadquery-skills]_  · `cadquery-skills/skills/cad-planner`
- **cadquery-export** — Export CadQuery models to STL/STEP/AMF/3MF and other formats for manufacturing, 3D printing, and visualization.  _[cadquery-skills]_  · `cadquery-skills/skills/cadquery-export`
- **design-versioning** — Track CAD design iterations with Git - commit changes, compare versions, maintain history like code.  _[cadquery-skills]_  · `cadquery-skills/skills/design-versioning`
- **export-paper-model** — Add-on de Blender (Addam Dominec, GPL-3.0+): papercraft — despliega la malla activa en una red imprimible con pestañas de pegado, numeración de bordes, pliegues valle/montaña, nesting en hojas; exporta SVG/PDF. Requiere Blender ≥5.0; se instala como extensión y se maneja vía Blender MCP.  _[tool]_  · `Export-Paper-Model-from-Blender`
- **boxes.py** — Boxes.py (florianfesti, GPL-3.0): 100+ generadores paramétricos de cajas/estructuras para corte láser — finger joints, ranura-lengüeta, living hinges, compensación de kerf; CLI/web → SVG. Requiere Python + instalar el paquete.  _[tool]_  · `boxes`
- **packbox-studio** — PackBox Studio (sbarrak, MIT): app local de empaques — dieline paramétrica, editor de arte, preview 3D del plegado. Electron/Vite, requiere Node.  _[tool]_  · `packbox`
- **packaging-dieline-generator** — Generador offline de dielines de cajas (NealZhouPanda, MIT): medidas + grosor de cartón → SVG 1:1 mm o PDF. Un solo HTML (release/), sin instalar.  _[tool]_  · `packaging-dieline-generator`
